			<article id="post-13159" class="entry content-bg single-entry post-13159 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-index category-animals category-easy category-educator-lib category-library category-most-popular category-origami-birds category-origami-cranes category-traditional-origami difficulty-beginner-level-3 folds-inside-reverse-fold folds-petal-fold folding_time-10-min bases-bird-base bases-square-base age-10-11 mv-content-wrapper">
	<div class="entry-content-wrap">
		<header class="entry-header post-title title-align-left title-tablet-align-inherit title-mobile-align-inherit">
	<h1 class="entry-title">How to Make an Origami Crane</h1><div class="entry-meta entry-meta-divider-dot">
	<span class="posted-by"><span class="author-avatar"><span class="author-image"><img alt='peter' src='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/peter-25x25.jpg' srcset='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/peter-50x50.jpg 2x' class='avatar avatar-25 photo' height='25' width='25' decoding='async'/></span><span class="image-size-ratio"></span></span><span class="meta-label">By</span><span class="author vcard"><span class="fn n">Peter Saydak</span></span></span><span class="posted-by"><span class="author-avatar designer-avatar"><span class="author-image designer-image"><img alt='crane origami icon' src='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/cropped-crane-origami-icon-50x50.png' srcset='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/cropped-crane-origami-icon-100x100.png 2x' class='avatar avatar-50 photo' height='50' width='50' decoding='async'/></span><span class="image-size-ratio"></span></span><span class="meta-label">Designer: </span><span class="designer vcard"><span class="fn n">Traditional</span></span></span><div class="meta-comments"><a class="meta-comments-link anchor-scroll" href="#comments">126 Comments</a></div>     </div><!-- .entry-meta -->
</header><!-- .entry-header -->

<div class="entry-content single-content">
	
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">If there’s one model that feels inseparable from origami, it’s the crane. In this tutorial, you’ll learn how to fold a traditional origami crane, the same way it has been folded in Japan for generations.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The crane is built from the bird base, one of the most important bases in origami. Once you learn it, you’ll start seeing it everywhere, in birds, animals, and many classic designs.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">In Japan, many children learn how to fold a crane in kindergarten or early elementary school. It’s not a difficult model, but it does require a bit of care and patience. If you take your time and follow the steps calmly, it’s a very approachable fold, even if you’re still fairly new to origami.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Here, we’ll go through the steps calmly and clearly, so you can focus on the process and end up with a balanced, elegant crane.</p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Traditional Origami Crane Step-by-Step Instructions</h2>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Difficulty</strong>: Easy &#8211; Beginner level 3</li>



<li><strong>Folding time</strong>: 5-10 minutes</li>
</ul>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-white-scallop has-theme-palette-7-background-color has-background"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<p class="is-style-narrow-bottom-margin wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Recommended Paper</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">6 x 6 in (15 x 15 cm) origami paper (Kami) or origami patterned paper (Chiyogami).</p>



<div class="wp-block-buttons is-layout-flex wp-block-buttons-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-button is-style-btn-white-arrow"><a class="wp-block-button__link has-theme-palette-9-color has-theme-palette-1-background-color has-text-color has-background has-link-color wp-element-button" href="https://amzn.to/4rSowEe" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener nofollow">Shop Paper</a></div>
</div>
</div></div>

<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">1</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">

<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1.png"><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20500%20500'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane diagram step 1" class="wp-image-1038948" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-100x100.png 100w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1.png" alt="origami crane diagram step 1" class="wp-image-1038948" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-1-100x100.png 100w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" /></noscript></a></figure>
</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Start with the colored side of the paper facing up in a diamond position. Fold and unfold along both diagonals (horizontal and vertical).</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">🕊️ <strong>Folding tip</strong>: The first creases are often the most important. Take your time and align everything carefully.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">2</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-2.gif"><img decoding="async" width="768" height="768" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20768%20768'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane paper fold step 2" class="wp-image-1038982" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-2.gif"/><noscript><img decoding="async" width="768" height="768" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-2.gif" alt="origami crane paper fold step 2" class="wp-image-1038982"/></noscript></a></figure>
</div>



<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2.png"><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20500%20500'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane diagram step 2" class="wp-image-1038949" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-100x100.png 100w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2.png" alt="origami crane diagram step 2" class="wp-image-1038949" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-2-100x100.png 100w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" /></noscript></a></figure>
</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Turn the paper over.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">3</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">

<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold and unfold the paper side to side, in both directions.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">4</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Collapse along the existing creases to form a Square base by bringing the left and right corners down to meet the bottom corner.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">

<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold the top flaps’ left and right edges to meet the vertical centerline.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">6</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow"><figure class="wp-block-jetpack-videopress pmd-svp" data-guid="9A9tctKh" style="position:relative;overflow:hidden;aspect-ratio:100 / 100;background:#000;margin-left:0;margin-right:0"><video class="pmd-svp-v" src="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/9A9tctKh/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-6_mp4_hd.mp4" poster="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/9A9tctKh/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-6_mp4_hd.original.jpg" data-alt="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/9A9tctKh/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-6_mp4_dvd.mp4|https://videos.files.wordpress.com/9A9tctKh/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-6.mp4" playsinline loop controls preload="none" aria-label="Video: origami-crane-paper-fold-step-6-mp4" style="display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover"></video><button type="button" class="pmd-svp-btn" aria-label="Play video"><svg width="28" height="28" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 5v14l11-7z" fill="currentColor"/></svg></button><div class="pmd-svp-track" role="slider" tabindex="0" aria-label="Video position" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="0"><span class="pmd-svp-bar"></span></div></figure></div>



<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold the top corner down, aligning it with the top edges of the flaps you just made in Step 5.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">7</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Unfold Step 5.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">

</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Lift the top layer and bring the bottom corner up to form a Petal fold. The outer edges will meet the vertical centerline.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">

</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Turn the model over.</p>
</div></div>

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10.png"><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20500%20500'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane diagram step 10" class="wp-image-1038958" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-100x100.png 100w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10.png" alt="origami crane diagram step 10" class="wp-image-1038958" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-10-100x100.png 100w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" /></noscript></a></figure>
</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold and unfold the left and right edges to meet the vertical centerline.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">11</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">
<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow"><figure class="wp-block-jetpack-videopress pmd-svp" data-guid="H5c7aQn1" style="position:relative;overflow:hidden;aspect-ratio:100 / 100;background:#000;margin-left:0;margin-right:0"><video class="pmd-svp-v" src="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/H5c7aQn1/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-11_mp4_hd.mp4" poster="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/H5c7aQn1/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-11_mp4_hd.original.jpg" data-alt="https://videos.files.wordpress.com/H5c7aQn1/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-11_mp4_dvd.mp4|https://videos.files.wordpress.com/H5c7aQn1/origami-crane-paper-fold-step-11.mp4" playsinline loop controls preload="none" aria-label="Video: origami-crane-paper-fold-step-11-mp4" style="display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover"></video><button type="button" class="pmd-svp-btn" aria-label="Play video"><svg width="28" height="28" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 5v14l11-7z" fill="currentColor"/></svg></button><div class="pmd-svp-track" role="slider" tabindex="0" aria-label="Video position" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="0"><span class="pmd-svp-bar"></span></div></figure></div>



<div class="wp-block-column is-layout-flow wp-block-column-is-layout-flow">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Just like in Step 8, lift the top layer and bring the bottom corner up to form another Petal fold.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">You now have a completed Bird base 🐦</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">12</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold the left and right edges to the vertical centerline.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">🕊️ <strong>Folding tip</strong>: Don’t fold right up to the centerline and leave a small gap. This will make the upcoming steps easier. Do the same in Step 14.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Turn the model over.</p>
</div></div>

<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">16</mark></h3>

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Fold the top right flap over to the left.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">🕊️ <strong>Folding tip</strong>: This is where those small gaps you left earlier come in handy. Things fold more smoothly now with all the layers involved.</p>
</div></div>

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.png"><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20500%20500'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane diagram step 26" class="wp-image-1038975" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-100x100.png 100w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.png" alt="origami crane diagram step 26" class="wp-image-1038975" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26-100x100.png 100w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" /></noscript></a></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><a href="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5.png"><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20500%20500'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="origami crane diagram step 26.5" class="wp-image-1038974" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-100x100.png 100w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5.png" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5.png" alt="origami crane diagram step 26.5" class="wp-image-1038974" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5.png 500w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-300x300.png 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-150x150.png 150w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-450x450.png 450w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-440x440.png 440w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/origami-crane-diagram-step-26.5-100x100.png 100w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" /></noscript></a></figure>
</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Mountain fold the crane’s head to the right. There’s no exact reference point here, so adjust the position to your liking. Crease well, then unfold.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">🕊️ <strong>Note</strong>: In the video, I used a valley fold. It won’t affect the next step. But if you&#8217;re folding in the air, a mountain fold is usually easier to manage.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">27</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

</div>
</div>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Push down the crane&#8217;s head, and inside reverse fold the head along the creases you just made.</p>
</div></div>



<div class="wp-block-group is-style-group-step"><div class="wp-block-group__inner-container is-layout-constrained wp-block-group-is-layout-constrained">
<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Step <mark style="background-color:var(--global-palette1)" class="has-inline-color">28</mark></h3>



<div class="wp-block-columns is-layout-flex wp-container-core-columns-is-layout-8f761849 wp-block-columns-is-layout-flex">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Gently open the wings.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">🕊️ <strong>Folding tip</strong>: You can round out the back of the crane by pulling the wings gently in opposite directions. Totally optional, but super satisfying 😊</p>
</div></div>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading">The Thousand Paper Cranes</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">In Japan, the crane is traditionally said to live for a thousand years. From this belief comes the idea that folding one thousand paper cranes can bring good fortune, healing, or the fulfillment of a wish. Over time, this story became one of the most well-known traditions connected to the origami crane.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The crane’s worldwide recognition is closely tied to <em><a href="https://amzn.to/3MoK6xc" target="_blank" data-type="link" data-id="https://amzn.to/3MoK6xc" rel="noreferrer noopener sponsored">Sadako and the Thousand Paper Cranes</a></em>, a children’s book written by Eleanor Coerr. It tells the story of Sadako Sasaki, a young Japanese girl who was exposed to radiation as a toddler during the Hiroshima bombing and later developed leukemia.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">While in the hospital, Sadako folded paper cranes, inspired by the hope that reaching one thousand would help her recover. According to her family, she folded well over a thousand cranes. After her death, her classmates continued folding cranes in her memory.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Today, paper cranes are widely used as symbols of peace and hope. They are often sent to memorials, museums, and remembrance sites around the world, where they stand not just for a single story, but for resilience, care, and the quiet power of a simple fold.</p>


<div class="wp-block-image">
<figure class="aligncenter size-full"><img decoding="async" width="1200" height="802" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201200%20802'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="1000 origami cranes" class="wp-image-522981" title="Origami Cranes" data-lazy-srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image.webp 1200w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-300x201.webp 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-1024x684.webp 1024w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-768x513.webp 768w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 1200px) 100vw, 1200px" data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image.webp" /><noscript><img decoding="async" width="1200" height="802" src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image.webp" alt="1000 origami cranes" class="wp-image-522981" title="Origami Cranes" srcset="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image.webp 1200w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-300x201.webp 300w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-1024x684.webp 1024w, https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/image-768x513.webp 768w" sizes="(max-width: 1200px) 100vw, 1200px" /></noscript></figure>
</div>


<p class="wp-block-paragraph">I hope you enjoyed folding this paper crane and taking the time to slow down with such a classic model. It’s one of those folds that becomes more satisfying each time you come back to it.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">If you make one, feel free to share a photo in the comments. Different papers, sizes, and small variations always give the crane a slightly different feel, and it’s always nice to see how others approach it.</p>

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Want to go further with <a href="https://origami.me/">origami</a>? You’ll find more ideas and techniques on our main hub.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">And if you’d like to stay connected, don’t forget to subscribe to our <a href="https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCFNQnw5TsJoO8__GPtJ6Lww?sub_confirmation=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener">origami YouTube channel</a> for step-by-step tutorials, and <a href="https://unfold.origami.me/">join our newsletter</a> to receive new models, tips, and inspiration straight to your inbox.</p>

							<span class="author-label">Designer</span>
							<b class="entry-author-name author-name fn"><a class="url fn n" href="https://origami.me/author/traditional/">Traditional</a></b>
														<div class="entry-author-description author-bio">
								<p>Traditional origami refers to classic models that are timeless and widely known. Usually passed down through generations, they belong to everyone and form the foundation of modern origami.</p>
							</div>
													</div>
					</div>
				</div>
			
							<div class="entry-diagrammer">
					<div class="entry-author entry-author-style-normal">
						<div class="entry-author-profile author-profile vcard">
							<div class="entry-author-avatar">
								<img alt='nick' src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%2080%2080'%3E%3C/svg%3E" data-lazy-srcset='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/nick-2-160x160.jpg 2x' class='avatar avatar-80 photo' height='80' width='80' decoding='async' data-lazy-src="https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/nick-2-80x80.jpg"/><noscript><img alt='nick' src='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/nick-2-80x80.jpg' srcset='https://origami.me/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/nick-2-160x160.jpg 2x' class='avatar avatar-80 photo' height='80' width='80' decoding='async'/></noscript>							</div>
							<span class="author-label">Diagrammer</span>
							<b class="entry-author-name author-name fn"><a class="url fn n" href="https://origami.me/author/nick-robinson/">Nick Robinson</a></b>
														<div class="entry-author-description author-bio">
								<p>Nick Robinson is a British origami artist, author of over 100 books, and former president of the British Origami Society. He’s been teaching paper folding for more than 40 years in schools, galleries, and online. His work focuses on clarity and simplicity, and when he’s not folding, he tours with his bands.</p>
							</div>

<script id="pmd-svp" data-noptimize="1" data-no-optimize="1" data-no-minify="1" data-no-defer="1" data-cfasync="false">
(function(){"use strict";try{
var D=document,all=[].slice.call(D.querySelectorAll(".pmd-svp"));
if(!all.length)return;
function isLoop(f){return f.classList.contains("pmd-svp--loop");}
var figs=all.filter(function(f){return !isLoop(f);}),loops=all.filter(isLoop);

// Fullscreen, for creases too small to read at column width (Flo, 2026-10-06:
// the VideoPress player had it, ours had lost it). The figure goes fullscreen
// so tap-to-pause and the scrubber keep working there. iPhone Safari has no
// element fullscreen, only the native video one, so it gets that instead.
// Corner button, 40 px, out of the middle of the frame.
var fsEl=function(){return D.fullscreenElement||D.webkitFullscreenElement||null;};
var canFs=!!(D.fullscreenEnabled||D.webkitFullscreenEnabled);
function addFs(f,v){
  var canIos=!canFs&&typeof v.webkitEnterFullscreen==="function";
  if(!canFs&&!canIos)return;
  var btn=D.createElement("button");
  btn.type="button";btn.className="pmd-svp-fs";btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Full screen");
  btn.innerHTML='<svg class="pmd-svp-fs-in" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9V4h5M20 9V4h-5M4 15v5h5M20 15v5h-5"/></svg>'
    +'<svg class="pmd-svp-fs-out" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9 4v5H4M15 4v5h5M9 20v-5H4M15 20v-5h5"/></svg>';
  f.appendChild(btn);
  btn.addEventListener("click",function(e){
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    if(v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}
    if(canIos){try{v.webkitEnterFullscreen();}catch(x){}return;}
    if(fsEl()===f){(D.exitFullscreen||D.webkitExitFullscreen).call(D);return;}
    var r=(f.requestFullscreen||f.webkitRequestFullscreen).call(f);
    if(r&&r["catch"])r["catch"](function(){});
  });
}
function onFs(){
  all.forEach(function(f){
    var on=fsEl()===f;f.classList.toggle("is-fs",on);
    var b=f.querySelector(".pmd-svp-fs");if(b)b.setAttribute("aria-label",on?"Exit full screen":"Full screen");
  });
}
D.addEventListener("fullscreenchange",onFs);
D.addEventListener("webkitfullscreenchange",onFs);

// Loops: the same rendition fallback, a tap to start them if the browser
// refused autoplay, and nothing else.
function setupLoop(f){
  var v=f.querySelector("video.pmd-svp-v");
  if(!v)return;
  v.muted=true;
  var alts=(v.getAttribute("data-alt")||"").split("|").filter(Boolean),tried=0;
  v.addEventListener("error",function(){
    if(tried<alts.length){v.src=alts[tried++];v.load();var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}
  });
  f.addEventListener("click",function(){if(v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}});
  addFs(f,v);
}
loops.forEach(setupLoop);

function setup(f){
  var v=f.querySelector("video.pmd-svp-v"),b=f.querySelector(".pmd-svp-btn"),bar=f.querySelector(".pmd-svp-bar"),track=f.querySelector(".pmd-svp-track");
  if(!v)return;
  v.removeAttribute("controls");
  f.classList.add("pmd-svp--js");
  addFs(f,v);

  // hd, then dvd, then the original upload. A rendition that is missing or
  // still encoding answers 404, which surfaces here as an error event.
  var alts=(v.getAttribute("data-alt")||"").split("|").filter(Boolean),tried=0;
  v.addEventListener("error",function(){
    if(tried<alts.length){
      var was=!v.paused;
      v.src=alts[tried++];v.load();
      if(was)v.play()["catch"](function(){});
    }else{
      // Out of options: hand the element back to the browser.
      f.classList.remove("pmd-svp--js","is-loading","is-playing");
      v.setAttribute("controls","");
    }
  });

  function toggle(){
    if(v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}
    else v.pause();
  }
  f.addEventListener("click",function(e){
    // Once the browser owns the controls again, leave every click to it.
    if(!f.classList.contains("pmd-svp--js"))return;
    e.preventDefault();toggle();
  });

  // The progress line is also the scrubber: press or drag anywhere along the
  // bottom strip to move, arrow keys nudge two seconds. Live only once the
  // clip has started (duration is unknown before that with preload=none),
  // and every event on it is stopped so the figure's tap-to-toggle never
  // fires from a scrub.
  function paint(){
    var p=v.duration>0?v.currentTime/v.duration:0;
    if(bar)bar.style.width=(p*100).toFixed(2)+"%";
    if(track)track.setAttribute("aria-valuenow",String(Math.round(p*100)));
  }
  function seekTo(x){
    if(!(v.duration>0))return;
    var r=track.getBoundingClientRect(),p=(x-r.left)/r.width;
    v.currentTime=Math.min(v.duration,Math.max(0,p))*v.duration;
    paint();
  }
  if(track&&window.PointerEvent){
    var drag=false;
    track.addEventListener("pointerdown",function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      drag=true;f.classList.add("is-scrubbing");
      try{track.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(x){}
      seekTo(e.clientX);
    });
    track.addEventListener("pointermove",function(e){if(drag)seekTo(e.clientX);});
    function end(){drag=false;f.classList.remove("is-scrubbing");}
    track.addEventListener("pointerup",end);
    track.addEventListener("pointercancel",end);
    track.addEventListener("click",function(e){e.stopPropagation();});
    track.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){
      var d=e.key==="ArrowLeft"?-2:e.key==="ArrowRight"?2:0;
      if(!d||!(v.duration>0))return;
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      v.currentTime=Math.min(v.duration,Math.max(0,v.currentTime+d));
      paint();
    });
  }

  v.addEventListener("play",function(){
    f.classList.add("is-playing","is-started");
    if(b)b.setAttribute("aria-label","Pause video");
    // One clip at a time: the reader is on one step, and a loop left running
    // three steps up is bandwidth for nothing.
    figs.forEach(function(o){if(o!==f){var ov=o.querySelector("video");if(ov&&!ov.paused)ov.pause();}});
  });
  v.addEventListener("pause",function(){
    f.classList.remove("is-playing","is-loading");
    if(b)b.setAttribute("aria-label","Play video");
  });
  v.addEventListener("loadstart",function(){if(!v.paused)f.classList.add("is-loading");});
  v.addEventListener("waiting",function(){f.classList.add("is-loading");});
  v.addEventListener("playing",function(){f.classList.remove("is-loading");});
  v.addEventListener("timeupdate",paint);
  v.addEventListener("seeked",paint);
}
figs.forEach(setup);

// Off screen, off. The reader scrolled on; do not keep pulling the file.
// Loops come back on by themselves when they scroll back in; step clips wait
// for a tap.
if("IntersectionObserver" in window){
  var io=new IntersectionObserver(function(es){
    es.forEach(function(en){
      var v=en.target.querySelector("video");
      if(!v)return;
      en.target._pmdIn=en.isIntersecting;
      if(en.isIntersecting){
        if(isLoop(en.target)&&v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}
        return;
      }
      if(!v.paused)v.pause();
    });
  },{threshold:0});
  all.forEach(function(f){io.observe(f);});
  // The browser pauses muted autoplay in a hidden tab (phone locked, app
  // switched); restart the loops in view when the page comes back.
  D.addEventListener("visibilitychange",function(){
    if(D.visibilityState!=="visible")return;
    loops.forEach(function(f){var v=f.querySelector("video");if(f._pmdIn&&v&&v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p["catch"])p["catch"](function(){});}});
  });
}
}catch(e){}})();
</script>

<script id="pmd-ilb-js">
(function(){
	var IMG = /\/wp-content\/uploads\/[^?#]+\.(jpe?g|png|gif|webp|avif)(\?[^#]*)?$/i;
	var box, img, closeBtn, current = null, lastFocus = null, pushed = false;

	function build(){
		box = document.createElement('div');
		box.className = 'pmd-ilb';
		box.setAttribute('role','dialog');
		box.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');
		box.setAttribute('aria-label','Image viewer');
		box.innerHTML =
			'<img class="pmd-ilb__img" alt="">' +
			'<button type="button" class="pmd-ilb__close"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg>Close</button>';
		document.body.appendChild(box);
		img = box.querySelector('.pmd-ilb__img');
		closeBtn = box.querySelector('.pmd-ilb__close');
		closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){ close(); });
		box.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === box) close(); });
	}

	function open(a){
		if (!box) build();
		var thumb = a.querySelector('img');
		var t = thumb && (thumb.currentSrc || thumb.src);
		current = a;
		lastFocus = document.activeElement;
		img.alt = (thumb && thumb.alt) || '';
		// Show the already-loaded thumbnail at once, swap to the full file when it arrives.
		if (t && t.indexOf('data:') !== 0) img.src = t;
		var full = new Image();
		full.onload = function(){ if (current === a) img.src = a.href; };
		full.src = a.href;
		box.classList.add('is-open');
		document.documentElement.classList.add('pmd-ilb-lock');
		closeBtn.focus({preventScroll:true});
		try { history.pushState({pmdIlb:1}, ''); pushed = true; } catch(e){ pushed = false; }
	}

	function close(fromPop){
		if (!box || !box.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
		box.classList.remove('is-open');
		document.documentElement.classList.remove('pmd-ilb-lock');
		img.removeAttribute('src');
		current = null;
		if (lastFocus && lastFocus.focus) lastFocus.focus({preventScroll:true});
		if (pushed && !fromPop){ pushed = false; history.back(); }
		pushed = false;
	}

	document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
		if (e.defaultPrevented || e.button !== 0 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return;
		var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href]');
		if (!a || !IMG.test(a.getAttribute('href')) || !a.querySelector('img')) return;
		if (!a.closest('.entry-content, .single-content')) return;
		e.preventDefault();
		open(a);
	});

	document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
		if (!box || !box.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
		if (e.key === 'Escape'){ e.preventDefault(); close(); }
		else if (e.key === 'Tab'){ e.preventDefault(); closeBtn.focus(); }
	});

	window.addEventListener('popstate', function(){ close(true); });
})();
</script>
	<script id="pmd-abp" data-noptimize="1" data-no-optimize="1" data-no-minify="1" data-no-defer="1" data-cfasync="false">
(function(){"use strict";try{
var C={"mode":"prompt","delay":20000,"cooldown":7,"max_shows":3,"patreon_url":"https:\/\/friends.origami.me\/join","buttons":"patreon","primary":"patreon","tally":"https:\/\/origami.me\/pmd-t.php?k=abp","overlap":"https:\/\/origami.me\/pmd-t.php?k=vpb","sample":0.1},K="pmd_abp",D=document,W=window,N=navigator;

/* --- bail out early, before any work ---------------------------------- */
if(N.webdriver)return;                                   // headless / automation
if(/Lighthouse|HeadlessChrome|GTmetrix|PageSpeed|Pingdom|bot|crawl|spider|preview/i.test(N.userAgent))return;
if(!W.fetch||!W.localStorage)return;                     // ancient browser, skip

var st;try{st=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(K)||"{}")}catch(e){st={}}

// The caps gate the modal only, never the detection. That way the ad block
// rate keeps being measured in both modes, and does not drift upward as
// readers get capped out of being asked.
var mayPrompt = C.mode!=="measure"
  && !st.ok                                              // whitelisted or opted out
  && (st.n||0)<C.max_shows
  && !(st.t&&Date.now()-st.t<C.cooldown*864e5);

/* --- schedule: C.delay of engaged time, accumulated across the visit --- */
// Counts total browsing time, not time on one page, so a reader who moves
// through five pages at 15s each still qualifies. Time only accrues while the
// tab is visible. sessionStorage scopes it to this visit; if it is unavailable
// the counter simply never persists and this degrades to the old per-page
// behaviour, which is safe.
var EK=K+"_e",FK=K+"_f",tick=null;
function ss(k,v){try{if(v===undefined)return sessionStorage.getItem(k);sessionStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}
var acc=parseInt(ss(EK)||"0",10)||0;
// Whether this visit is in the beacon sample. Drawn once and shared with the
// video snippet through sessionStorage, so the overlap pair is sampled as a unit.
function smp(){var v=ss("pmd_smp");if(v!=="1"&&v!=="0"){v=Math.random()<C.sample?"1":"0";ss("pmd_smp",v)}return v==="1"}
function fired(){return ss(FK)==="1"}

function arm(){
  // already counted this visit and cannot show the modal: nothing left to do
  if(fired()&&!mayPrompt)return;
  tick=setInterval(function(){
    if(D.hidden)return;                 // reader is not actually here
    acc+=1000;ss(EK,String(acc));
    if(acc>=C.delay){clearInterval(tick);tick=null;check()}
  },1000);
}
if(D.readyState==="complete"){arm()}else{W.addEventListener("load",arm,{once:true})}

/* --- detection: two independent signals, no libraries ----------------- */
function check(){
  var done=0,hit=false;
  var seal=function(h){if(h)hit=true;if(++done===2)settle()};
  var settle=function(){
    // Both outcomes are always reported, so blocked/(blocked+clear) is the true
    // ad block rate among engaged sessions, carrying its own denominator.
    // Tally once per visit, not once per page, so heavy readers do not get
    // weighted more than light ones and the denominator is genuinely sessions.
    if(!fired()){ss(FK,"1");track(hit?"blocked":"clear")}
    ss(RK,hit?"blocked":"clear");   // record for the overlap pairing
    overlap();
    if(hit&&mayPrompt)show();
  };

  // 1. cosmetic filters: class names every major filter list hides
  var b=D.createElement("div");
  b.className="adsbygoogle ad-placement ad-banner adsbox pub_300x250 text-ad";
  b.style.cssText="position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;width:1px;height:1px;pointer-events:none";
  b.innerHTML="&nbsp;";
  D.body.appendChild(b);
  setTimeout(function(){
    var cs=getComputedStyle(b);
    var blocked=b.offsetHeight===0||b.offsetParent===null||cs.display==="none"||cs.visibility==="hidden";
    b.remove();seal(blocked);
  },150);

  // 2. network filters: probe the real Mediavine tag (already in HTTP cache)
  fetch("https://scripts.mediavine.com/tags/origami-dot-me.js",
        {method:"HEAD",mode:"no-cors",cache:"force-cache"})
    .then(function(){seal(false)})
    .catch(function(){seal(true)});
}

/* --- reporting -------------------------------------------------------- */
function track(a){
  try{
    // EVERY action goes first-party, not just detection. GTM and GA4 are
    // google-hosted, so they are blocked for exactly the readers who see this
    // modal: conversion measured through GA4 alone would read as roughly zero
    // however well the ask performed, which is worse than no number at all.
    // dataLayer stays for the clear-path readers, where GA4 does work.
    // The verdicts go out from the sampled visits only; interactions always do.
    if(N.sendBeacon&&(a!=="blocked"&&a!=="clear"||smp()))N.sendBeacon(C.tally+"&s="+a);
    (W.dataLayer=W.dataLayer||[]).push({event:"adblock_prompt",abp_action:a});
    if(typeof W.gtag==="function")W.gtag("event","adblock_prompt",{action:a});
  }catch(e){}
}
function save(o){try{localStorage.setItem(K,JSON.stringify(o))}catch(e){}}

/* --- overlap with the video block verdict ------------------------------ */
// Mirror of the pairing in the video snippet. Each side records its own verdict
// and whichever settles SECOND emits the pair, so neither has to wait on the
// other. This side is usually second: it needs 20s of engaged time, while the
// video probe settles about a second after load.
var RK="pmd_abp_r",VK="pmd_vpb_r",OVK="pmd_ov_f";
function overlap(){
  try{
    if(ss(OVK)==="1")return;                 // already paired this visit
    var a=ss(RK),v=ss(VK);
    if(!a||!v)return;                        // no video verdict (page had none)
    ss(OVK,"1");
    var s="ov_ad"+a+"_video"+v;
    // The country bucket is computed by the video snippet (from the device
    // timezone) and left in sessionStorage, so whichever side emits the pair
    // tags it identically. Absent on pages with no video, which is harmless:
    // the receiving route only records geo for whitelisted states anyway.
    var cc=ss("pmd_cc");
    if(N.sendBeacon&&smp())N.sendBeacon(C.overlap+"&s="+s+(cc?"&c="+cc:""));
    (W.dataLayer=W.dataLayer||[]).push({event:"adblock_prompt",abp_action:s});
  }catch(e){}
}

    wl+pt+ht+

  // browser stalls the transition (throttled tab, no compositing)
  setTimeout(function(){
    if(parseFloat(getComputedStyle(o).opacity)<.9){o.style.transition="none";o.style.opacity="1"}
  },400);

  var prev=D.activeElement;
  D.getElementById("abp-x").focus();
  save({n:(st.n||0)+1,t:Date.now()});
  track("shown");

  function close(a){
    track(a);o.style.opacity="";o.style.transition="";o.classList.remove("on");
    setTimeout(function(){o.remove();s.remove();if(prev&&prev.focus)prev.focus()},260);
    D.removeEventListener("keydown",key);
  }
  function key(e){
    if(e.key==="Escape")close("dismiss");
    if(e.key==="Tab"){ // simple focus trap
      var f=o.querySelectorAll("button,a[href]");
      if(!f.length)return;
      var a=f[0],z=f[f.length-1];
      if(e.shiftKey&&D.activeElement===a){e.preventDefault();z.focus()}
      else if(!e.shiftKey&&D.activeElement===z){e.preventDefault();a.focus()}
    }
  }
  D.addEventListener("keydown",key);
  D.getElementById("abp-x").onclick=function(){close("dismiss")};
  D.getElementById("abp-n").onclick=function(){close("dismiss")};
  o.onclick=function(e){if(e.target===o)close("dismiss")};
  var w=D.getElementById("abp-w");
  if(w)w.onclick=function(){
    D.getElementById("abp-h").classList.add("on");
    this.textContent="Done, I’ve turned it off";
    this.onclick=function(){save({n:st.n||1,t:Date.now(),ok:1});close("whitelist")};
    track("howto");
  };
  var p=D.getElementById("abp-p");
  if(p)p.onclick=function(){save({n:st.n||1,t:Date.now(),ok:1});track("patreon")};
}
}catch(e){}})();

	<script id="pmd-vpb" data-noptimize="1" data-no-optimize="1" data-no-minify="1" data-no-defer="1" data-cfasync="false">
(function(){"use strict";try{
var C={"delay":1000,"timeout":5000,"youtube":"https:\/\/www.youtube.com\/watch?v=m-58IsUcvfA","tally":"https:\/\/origami.me\/pmd-t.php?k=vpb","sample":0.1},D=document,W=window,N=navigator,K="pmd_vpb_f";

/* --- bail out early, before any work ---------------------------------- */
// Search engines must never see this notice. It is injected client-side and
// Googlebot renders JavaScript, so an unrendered or slow videopress.com fetch
// inside Google's renderer would otherwise stamp "Having trouble with the
// videos?" across the page 22 times. Treating silence as blocked (correct for
// school firewalls, see probe below) makes that MORE likely, not less, which is
// why this list is explicit rather than relying on /bot/ alone:
// Google-InspectionTool, GoogleOther and Chrome-Lighthouse contain no "bot",
// "crawl" or "spider" and would sail straight through a generic pattern.
var UA=N.userAgent||"";
if(!UA)return;
if(N.webdriver)return;
if(D.prerendering)return;
if(/Googlebot|Google-InspectionTool|GoogleOther|Storebot|AdsBot|Mediapartners|APIs-Google/i.test(UA))return;
if(/bingbot|BingPreview|Slurp|DuckDuckBot|Baiduspider|YandexBot|Applebot|PetalBot|Bytespider/i.test(UA))return;
if(/facebookexternalhit|Twitterbot|LinkedInBot|Pinterest|WhatsApp|TelegramBot|Discordbot|Slackbot|redditbot|Embedly|Quora/i.test(UA))return;
if(/SemrushBot|AhrefsBot|DotBot|MJ12bot|DataForSeo|Screaming Frog|ia_archiver|archive\.org/i.test(UA))return;
if(/Lighthouse|HeadlessChrome|GTmetrix|PageSpeed|Pingdom|WebPageTest|bot|crawl|spider|preview/i.test(UA))return;
if(!W.fetch)return;

// Confirmed against the live Dragon page: 22 iframes, every one with a real
// src of https://videopress.com/embed/<guid>. No lazy-loading, no data-src.
// Since the step video player (90-lab/step-video-player) most blocks render
// as a native <video> whose src is the MP4 on videos.files.wordpress.com; the
// iframe survives only for clips the player could not resolve. Both count.
var SEL='iframe[src*="videopress.com"],iframe[src*="video.wordpress.com"],video.pmd-svp-v';
function frames(){return [].slice.call(D.querySelectorAll(SEL))}

function ss(k,v){try{if(v===undefined)return sessionStorage.getItem(k);sessionStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}

/* --- country bucket ---------------------------------------------------- */
// From the device timezone, not the IP. A large share of this cohort is on a
// VPN, where the IP reports the exit node and would systematically misplace
// exactly the readers being studied; the device timezone still says where they
// are. It also needs no IP handling and no geo service.
//
// Bucketed to seven values, never the raw zone: enough to separate the markets
// that carry the revenue (US session RPM $174 against India's $3.62) without
// storing anything that could single a reader out.
function geo(){
  try{
    var z=(Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone||"");
    if(!z)return "other";
    if(/^Australia\//.test(z))return "au";
    if(z==="Asia/Kolkata"||z==="Asia/Calcutta")return "in";
    if(z==="Europe/London"||z==="Europe/Belfast")return "uk";
    if(z==="Pacific/Honolulu")return "us";
    if(/^America\/(New_York|Chicago|Denver|Los_Angeles|Phoenix|Anchorage|Detroit|Boise|Juneau|Sitka|Nome|Adak|Menominee|Indiana|Kentucky|North_Dakota)/.test(z))return "us";
    if(/^America\/(Toronto|Vancouver|Edmonton|Winnipeg|Halifax|St_Johns|Regina|Moncton|Montreal|Whitehorse|Yellowknife|Iqaluit|Glace_Bay|Goose_Bay|Swift_Current|Dawson|Inuvik|Rankin_Inlet|Resolute|Cambridge_Bay|Fort_Nelson|Blanc-Sablon|Atikokan|Thunder_Bay|Rainy_River|Pangnirtung)/.test(z))return "ca";
    if(/^Europe\//.test(z))return "eu";
    return "other";
  }catch(e){return "other"}
}
var CC=geo();
ss("pmd_cc",CC);   // so the ad block snippet can tag the overlap it emits

// Whether this visit is in the beacon sample. Drawn once and shared with the ad
// block snippet through sessionStorage, so the overlap pair is sampled as a unit.
function smp(){var v=ss("pmd_smp");if(v!=="1"&&v!=="0"){v=Math.random()<C.sample?"1":"0";ss("pmd_smp",v)}return v==="1"}

/* --- reporting -------------------------------------------------------- */
// Once per visit, so the number answers "how many readers hit this", not
// "how many tutorial pages did they open". Same shape as the ad block tally,
// and first-party for the same reason: the readers being counted are the ones
// whose network blocks third-party domains.
function track(s){
  try{
    if(ss(K)==="1")return;
    ss(K,"1");
    if(N.sendBeacon&&smp())N.sendBeacon(C.tally+"&s="+s+"&c="+CC);
    (W.dataLayer=W.dataLayer||[]).push({event:"video_block",vpb_action:s});
  }catch(e){}
}

/* --- overlap with the ad block verdict --------------------------------- */
// The decisive number: are video-blocked readers also ad-blocked? If they are,
// they were already earning nothing and a broken video costs no revenue.
//
// Neither snippet can answer this alone. This one settles about a second after
// load; the ad block check needs 20s of cumulative engaged time. So each writes
// its own verdict to sessionStorage and whichever finishes SECOND emits the
// pair. Order does not matter and no waiting is involved.
//
// Denominator is therefore visits where BOTH verdicts exist. A reader who never
// reaches 20s of engaged time is counted by neither, which is correct: we do
// not know their ad status, so we cannot place them.
var RK="pmd_vpb_r",AK="pmd_abp_r",OVK="pmd_ov_f",OK="pmd_vpb_o",YK="pmd_vpb_y";

// Fire a state at most once per visit. Used for the interaction signals, which
// would otherwise be counted per click and let one reader skew the number.
function once(flag,state){
  try{
    if(ss(flag)==="1")return;
    ss(flag,"1");
    if(N.sendBeacon)N.sendBeacon(C.tally+"&s="+state+"&c="+CC);
    (W.dataLayer=W.dataLayer||[]).push({event:"video_block",vpb_action:state});
  }catch(e){}
}
function overlap(){
  try{
    if(ss(OVK)==="1")return;                 // already paired this visit
    var v=ss(RK),a=ss(AK);
    if(!v||!a)return;                        // the other half has not landed yet
    ss(OVK,"1");
    var s="ov_ad"+a+"_video"+v;
    if(N.sendBeacon&&smp())N.sendBeacon(C.tally+"&s="+s+"&c="+CC);
    (W.dataLayer=W.dataLayer||[]).push({event:"video_block",vpb_action:s});
  }catch(e){}
}

/* --- the notice ------------------------------------------------------- */
// Collapsed by default: a page can carry 22 of these (37 on dragon-advanced),
// and the full text repeated that many times would swamp the tutorial. Only the
// question shows; the answer is one click away. Native <details> rather than a
// hand-rolled toggle, so keyboard access, screen readers and the open/close
// state all come for free with no event handlers to bind.
function body(){
  var p='<p>Something on your network may be blocking them, such as a VPN, ad blocker, or school or office Wi-Fi filter.</p>'+
        '<p>Try switching off your VPN or ad blocker and reloading the page. You can also open the tutorial on your phone or try a different connection.</p>';
  if(C.youtube)p+='<p>This tutorial also has a full-length video walkthrough on YouTube: '+
    '<a href="'+C.youtube+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">watch it here</a>.</p>';
  return '<summary>Videos are not loading</summary><div class="pmd-vpb-b">'+p+'</div>';
}

function css(){
  var s=D.createElement("style");
  s.textContent=
  // The slot keeps the player's footprint. Without it the reader gets a bare
  // line asking about videos with no video anywhere on screen, and no way to
  // tell what it refers to (Flo, 2026-08-27).
  '.pmd-vpb-slot{background:#ECE2CE;border-radius:10px;padding:16px;margin:10px 0;'+
  'display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;'+
  'gap:14px;width:100%;box-sizing:border-box}'+
  '.pmd-vpb-slot *{box-sizing:border-box}'+
  '.pmd-vpb-ico{color:#B3A184;line-height:0;flex:none}'+
  '.pmd-vpb{background:rgba(255,255,255,.62);color:#343434;border-radius:9px;'+
  'padding:12px 16px;font-family:"Segoe UI",system-ui,sans-serif;font-size:15px;'+
  'line-height:1.6;width:100%;max-width:430px;margin:0;box-sizing:border-box}'+
  '.pmd-vpb *{box-sizing:border-box}'+
  // list-style:none + the webkit marker rule kill the default disclosure
  // triangle in every engine; the +/- is drawn on the right instead.
  '.pmd-vpb>summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:#C23F49;list-style:none;'+
  'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}'+
  '.pmd-vpb>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}'+
  '.pmd-vpb>summary::after{content:"+";margin-left:auto;font-size:19px;line-height:1;opacity:.7}'+
  '.pmd-vpb[open]>summary::after{content:"–"}'+
  '.pmd-vpb>summary:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #51A59F;outline-offset:3px}'+
  '.pmd-vpb .pmd-vpb-b{margin-top:11px}'+
  '.pmd-vpb p{margin:0 0 10px}.pmd-vpb p:last-child{margin:0}'+
  '.pmd-vpb a{color:#51A59F}'+
  '@media(max-width:480px){.pmd-vpb-slot{padding:12px;gap:10px}'+
  '.pmd-vpb{padding:11px 14px;font-size:14.5px}}';
  D.head.appendChild(s);
}

// Replace EVERY blocked player, not one banner at the top of the page. A reader
// who skips the easy early steps and lands on step 14 has to meet the
// explanation there. Dragon carries 22 of these, dragon-advanced 37.
//
// The replacement keeps the player's footprint, with a video glyph, so it still
// reads as "a video belongs here". Hiding the player and leaving only a line of
// text was confusing: nothing on screen said which video it referred to
// (Flo, 2026-08-27). The notice inside stays collapsed, so the page is no
// longer than a normal tutorial, it just has placeholders where players were.
var ICO='<svg width="34" height="34" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" '+
  'stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">'+
  '<rect x="2" y="4" width="20" height="16" rx="3"></rect>'+
  '<path d="M10 9l5 3-5 3z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"></path></svg>';

function swap(){
  var f=frames();
  if(!f.length)return;
  css();
  f.forEach(function(el){
    var h=Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().height)||
          parseInt(el.getAttribute("height"),10)||0;
    var slot=D.createElement("div");
    slot.className="pmd-vpb-slot";
    // Match the player so the step layout does not collapse or jump.
    if(h>80)slot.style.minHeight=h+"px";
    slot.innerHTML='<div class="pmd-vpb-ico" aria-hidden="true">'+ICO+'</div>'+
                   '<details class="pmd-vpb">'+body()+'</details>';
    // A native player is a <figure> carrying the video, its badge and its
    // progress bar; hide the whole thing, not just the <video> inside it.
    var host=(el.closest&&el.closest(".pmd-svp"))||el;
    host.style.display="none";
    if(host.parentNode)host.parentNode.insertBefore(slot,host);

    // Expanding the notice is an explicit signal that this reader went looking
    // for the video, which is the subset a loss estimate should be built on.
    // `toggle` does not bubble, so bind per element rather than delegating.
    var d=slot.querySelector("details");
    if(d)d.addEventListener("toggle",function(){if(d.open)once(OK,"notice_open")});
    var a=slot.querySelector("a");
    if(a)a.addEventListener("click",function(){once(YK,"notice_youtube")});
  });
}

/* --- detection -------------------------------------------------------- */
// This REPLACES the player, so a false positive hides a working video from a
// reader who had no problem. That is strictly worse than doing nothing: a
// missed block leaves them exactly where they are today, seeing the raw
// failure. So the bar for replacing is positive evidence, never a guess.
//
// Three probes, and the notice appears only on an unambiguous verdict:
//
//   control  same-origin. Must SUCCEED. If our own domain is unreachable the
//            reader's connection is broken in general, and telling them to turn
//            off a VPN would be wrong. Say nothing.
//   player   videopress.com, read off the iframe rather than hardcoded.
//   media    videos.files.wordpress.com, where the actual video file lives.
//            Worth probing separately: the reported "This video is private"
//            case had the PLAYER loading fine and the media host blocked.
//
// A timeout is deliberately NOT treated as blocked. An earlier version did, to
// catch firewalls that drop packets silently, but under a design that replaces
// the player that same rule would hide working videos from anyone on a slow
// connection. Inconclusive means leave the page alone and report nothing.
function probe(){
  var f=frames();
  if(!f.length)return;              // no video on this page, nothing to say
  if(N.onLine===false)return;       // offline: everything fails, say nothing

  var settled=false;
  function done(state){
    if(settled)return;settled=true;
    if(!state)return;               // inconclusive: touch nothing, count nothing
    track(state);
    ss(RK,state);                   // record for the overlap pairing
    overlap();
    if(state==="blocked")swap();
  }

  // Resolves to "ok" | "fail" | "timeout". Never rejects.
  function reach(url,opts){
    return new Promise(function(res){
      var timer=setTimeout(function(){res("timeout")},C.timeout);
      fetch(url,opts).then(function(){clearTimeout(timer);res("ok")},
                           function(){clearTimeout(timer);res("fail")});
    });
  }

  Promise.all([
    reach(location.origin+"/favicon.ico?vpb="+Date.now(),{method:"HEAD",cache:"no-store"}),
    reach(f[0].src,{method:"HEAD",mode:"no-cors",cache:"force-cache"}),
    reach("https://videos.files.wordpress.com/",{method:"HEAD",mode:"no-cors",cache:"no-store"})
  ]).then(function(r){
    var control=r[0],player=r[1],media=r[2];
    if(control!=="ok")      return done(null);      // their network, not our story
    if(player==="fail"||media==="fail") return done("blocked");
    if(player==="ok"&&media==="ok")     return done("clear");
    return done(null);                              // a timeout somewhere: unknown
  });
}

function arm(){setTimeout(probe,C.delay)}
if(D.readyState==="complete"){arm()}else{W.addEventListener("load",arm,{once:true})}
}catch(e){}})();
</script>

	<script id="pmd-fpr" data-noptimize="1" data-no-optimize="1" data-no-defer="1" data-cfasync="false">
(function(){
	var CODES = ["ITXdNg","pbpfjD","ljrjd4","VT9iPP","OTbKw4","U7yQUO","ypfVjX","oTXJ4L","9BW1pP","SHLRY2","uOivgE","2RJkn1","0OmY6R"];
	var URL = "https:\/\/origami.me\/wp-json\/pmd\/v1\/fpr";
	var seen = {};
	document.addEventListener('submit', function(e){
		try {
			var f = e.target, box = f && f.closest && f.closest('.ml-embedded[data-form]');
			if (!box) return;
			var code = box.getAttribute('data-form');
			if (CODES.indexOf(code) < 0) return;
			var inp = f.querySelector('input[type="email"], input[name="fields[email]"]');
			var email = inp && inp.value.trim();
			if (!email || !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email)) return;
			if (seen[code + email]) return;
			seen[code + email] = 1;
			fetch(URL, {method:'POST', keepalive:true, credentials:'omit',
				headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
				body: JSON.stringify({form: code, email: email})}).catch(function(){});
		} catch(x) {}
	}, true);
})();
